```
1	use std::hint::black_box;
2	use std::sync::mpsc;
3	
4	use crate::harness::{self, Bench, RunCfg};
5	
6	pub const NAME: &str = "mpsc-1t";
7	
8	pub struct StdMpscRoundTrip {
9	    tx: mpsc::Sender<u64>,
10	    rx: mpsc::Receiver<u64>,
11	    counter: u64,
12	}
13	
14	impl StdMpscRoundTrip {
15	    pub fn new() -> Self {
16	        let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel();
17	        Self { tx, rx, counter: 0 }
18	    }
19	}
20	
21	impl Bench for StdMpscRoundTrip {
22	    fn name(&self) -> &str {
23	        "std::sync::mpsc round-trip (1 thread)"
24	    }
25	
26	    fn step(&mut self) -> u64 {
27	        self.counter = self.counter.wrapping_add(1);
28	        self.tx.send(self.counter).unwrap();
29	        let v = self.rx.recv().unwrap();
30	        black_box(v)
31	    }
32	}
33	
34	pub fn run(cfg: &RunCfg) {
35	    let mut bench = StdMpscRoundTrip::new();
36	    let (hist, iterations, inner, duration_s) = harness::run_adaptive(&mut bench, cfg);
37	    harness::print_histogram(
38	        bench.name(),
39	        iterations,
40	        inner,
41	        duration_s,
42	        &hist,
43	        cfg.overhead,
44	    );
45	}
46
```

```
450	### Root cause
451	
452	`pin_current` called `get_core_ids()` to find the `CoreId` matching
453	the target, then passed it to `set_for_current`. But `get_core_ids()`
454	reflects the inherited affinity mask (which the main thread narrowed
455	to one core before spawning workers). The target core was absent
456	from the list, so `find()` returned `None` and no pinning happened.
457	
458	### Fix
459	
460	Construct `CoreId { id: target }` directly instead of looking it up
461	via `get_core_ids()`. The struct is just a `usize` wrapper — no OS
462	query needed.
463	
464	### Verification
465	
466	Before fix: `--pin 6,10` → worker printed `ids=[CoreId { id: 6 }]`,
467	`after pin_current: cid=6` (still on main's core).
468	
469	After fix: `--pin 5,10` → worker printed
470	`set_for_current(CoreId { id: 10 })`, `after pin_current: cid=10`
471	(correct core). System Monitor confirmed both target cores active.
472	
473	Observation: ~60% utilization per core is expected — mpsc ping-pong
474	means each thread alternates between sending and blocking on recv.
475	`user=4.6s` vs `sys=15.6s` over 19.6s confirms most time is in
476	kernel futex wake/wait, not userspace.
477	
478	Multi-step: 0.3.6-dbg-core-affinity retained debug prints,
479	0.3.6 cleans up: removed all debug prints from pin_current and
480	mpsc_2t (run, new, drop). Moved `cid` → `pin::print_core_id`
481	(pub, `#[allow(dead_code)]`) as a reusable debug helper that
482	prints to stderr via `sched_getcpu`; used only in the
483	`pin_current_can_switch_cores` test. Kept `derive(Debug)` on
484	`RunCfg` and `Overhead` — zero-cost when unused, useful for
485	future debugging.
486	
```

> DEVELOPER

On to 0.4.0 one other change I like a nother column in the output which is the "range", i.e. `last - first + 1` to give a more complete picture, thoughts

> AGENT

Good idea — `range` shows band spread at a glance without mental subtraction. A tight range means the band is clustered; a wide range means high variance within that percentile slice. Especially useful for spotting bimodal behavior in min-p1 or p99-max.

I'd put it after `last` and before `count`:

```
first         last        range        count      mean     adjusted
```

The `+1` makes sense for integer ns values — `first=100, last=100` → `range=1` (one distinct value, not zero spread).

For the summary rows (mean, stdev) the range column would just be blank since those are whole-histogram stats.

Single-step 0.4.0 with both changes (time-based outer + range column)?

```
24	    pub outer_override: Option<u64>,
25	    pub inner_override: Option<u64>,
26	    /// Core pool for thread pinning. Indexed positionally with wrap-around
27	    /// via `core_for(thread_idx)`; empty means no pinning.
28	    pub pin_cores: &'a [usize],
29	}
30	
31	impl RunCfg<'_> {
32	    /// CPU id for the bench's `thread_idx`-th thread, using wrap-around
33	    /// over the pool. Returns `None` when the pool is empty so callers
34	    /// can treat unpinned and pinned runs uniformly.
35	    pub fn core_for(&self, thread_idx: usize) -> Option<usize> {
36	        if self.pin_cores.is_empty() {
37	            None
38	        } else {
39	            Some(self.pin_cores[thread_idx % self.pin_cores.len()])
40	        }
41	    }
42	}
43	
44	pub fn run_adaptive<B: Bench>(bench: &mut B, cfg: &RunCfg) -> (Histogram<u64>, u64, u64, f64) {
45	    for _ in 0..WARMUP {
46	        black_box(bench.step());
47	    }
48	
49	    let step_cost_ns = estimate_step_cost(bench);
50	    let framing_ns = cfg.overhead.framing_per_sample_ns.max(1.0);
51	    let inner = cfg
52	        .inner_override
53	        .unwrap_or_else(|| pick_inner(step_cost_ns, framing_ns));
54	    let outer = cfg
55	        .outer_override
56	        .unwrap_or_else(|| pick_outer(step_cost_ns, inner, cfg.target_seconds));
57	
58	    let (hist, duration_s) = run_bench(bench, outer, inner);
59	    (hist, outer, inner, duration_s)
60	}
61	
62	fn estimate_step_cost<B: Bench>(bench: &mut B) -> f64 {
63	    let mut samples: Vec<f64> = (0..ESTIMATE_SAMPLES)
64	        .map(|_| {
65	            let start = minstant::Instant::now();
66	            for _ in 0..ESTIMATE_STEPS {
67	                black_box(bench.step());
68	            }
69	            let total = start.elapsed().as_nanos() as u64;
70	            total as f64 / ESTIMATE_STEPS as f64
71	        })
72	        .collect();
73	    samples.sort_by(|a, b| a.partial_cmp(b).unwrap());
74	    samples[ESTIMATE_SAMPLES / 2]
75	}
76	
77	fn pick_inner(step_cost_ns: f64, framing_ns: f64) -> u64 {
78	    let target = (FRAMING_DOMINATION_RATIO * framing_ns / step_cost_ns).ceil() as u64;
79	    target.clamp(1, MAX_INNER)
80	}
81	
82	fn pick_outer(step_cost_ns: f64, inner: u64, target_seconds: f64) -> u64 {
83	    let target_ns = target_seconds * 1e9;
84	    let per_sample_ns = inner as f64 * step_cost_ns;
85	    let raw = (target_ns / per_sample_ns).ceil() as u64;
86	    raw.clamp(MIN_OUTER, MAX_OUTER)
87	}
88	
89	fn run_bench<B: Bench>(bench: &mut B, outer: u64, inner: u64) -> (Histogram<u64>, f64) {
90	    // 1 ns to 60 s, 3 sig figs
91	    let mut hist = Histogram::<u64>::new_with_bounds(1, 60_000_000_000, 3).unwrap();
92	
93	    let run_start = minstant::Instant::now();
94	    for _ in 0..outer {
95	        let start = minstant::Instant::now();
96	        for _ in 0..inner {
97	            black_box(bench.step());
98	        }
99	        let elapsed_ns = start.elapsed().as_nanos() as u64;
100	        hist.record(round_elapsed(elapsed_ns, inner)).unwrap();
101	    }
102	    let duration_s = run_start.elapsed().as_nanos() as f64 / 1e9;
103	
104	    (hist, duration_s)
105	}
106	
107	fn round_elapsed(elapsed_ns: u64, inner: u64) -> u64 {
108	    (elapsed_ns + inner / 2) / inner
109	}
110	
111	pub fn fmt_commas(n: u64) -> String {
112	    let s = n.to_string();
113	    let mut result = String::new();
114	    for (i, c) in s.chars().rev().enumerate() {
115	        if i > 0 && i % 3 == 0 {
116	            result.push(',');
117	        }
118	        result.push(c);
119	    }
120	    result.chars().rev().collect()
121	}
122	
123	pub fn fmt_commas_f64(n: f64, decimals: usize) -> String {
124	    let s = format!("{n:.decimals$}");
125	    let (sign, body) = match s.strip_prefix('-') {
126	        Some(rest) => ("-", rest),
127	        None => ("", s.as_str()),
128	    };
129	    let (int_part, frac_part) = match body.find('.') {
130	        Some(i) => (&body[..i], &body[i..]),
131	        None => (body, ""),
132	    };
133	    let int_num: u64 = int_part.parse().unwrap_or(0);
134	    format!("{sign}{}{frac_part}", fmt_commas(int_num))
135	}
136	
137	pub fn print_histogram(
138	    name: &str,
139	    outer: u64,
140	    inner: u64,
141	    duration_s: f64,
142	    hist: &Histogram<u64>,
143	    overhead: &Overhead,
144	) {
145	    // Header line: bench name + logfmt-style metadata. `adj` is the
146	    // apparatus overhead subtracted from each sample downstream.
147	    let adj = overhead.per_call_ns(inner);
148	    let total = outer * inner;
149	    println!(
150	        "{name} [duration={:.1}s outer={} inner={} calls={} adj/call={}ns]:",
151	        duration_s,
152	        fmt_commas(outer),
153	        inner,
154	        fmt_commas(total),
155	        fmt_commas_f64(adj, 2),
156	    );
157	
158	    // Band boundaries defined by percentiles. Each consecutive pair
159	    // forms one band; we iterate the histogram to compute per-band
160	    // stats (first, last, count, mean).
161	    let boundary_pcts: &[f64] = &[
162	        0.0, 0.01, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.40, 0.50, 0.60, 0.70, 0.80, 0.90, 0.99, 1.0,
163	    ];
164	    let boundary_names: &[&str] = &[
165	        "min", "p1", "p10", "p20", "p30", "p40", "p50", "p60", "p70", "p80", "p90", "p99", "max",
166	    ];
167	
168	    let n_bands = boundary_pcts.len() - 1;
169	    let sample_count = hist.len();
170	
171	    // Accumulate per-band stats by walking recorded histogram buckets.
172	    // Each bucket is assigned to the band containing its midpoint rank.
173	    let mut band_first = vec![u64::MAX; n_bands];
174	    let mut band_last = vec![0u64; n_bands];
175	    let mut band_count = vec![0u64; n_bands];
176	    let mut band_sum = vec![0u128; n_bands];
177	
178	    let mut cumulative = 0u64;
179	    for iv in hist.iter_recorded() {
180	        let value = iv.value_iterated_to();
181	        let count = iv.count_at_value();
182	        let mid_rank = (cumulative as f64 + count as f64 / 2.0) / sample_count as f64;
183	        let idx = boundary_pcts[1..]
184	            .iter()
185	            .position(|&b| mid_rank < b)
186	            .unwrap_or(n_bands - 1);
187	        band_first[idx] = band_first[idx].min(value);
188	        band_last[idx] = band_last[idx].max(value);
189	        band_count[idx] += count;
190	        band_sum[idx] += value as u128 * count as u128;
191	        cumulative += count;
192	    }
193	
194	    // Build rendered rows: (label, first, last, count, mean, adj_mean).
195	    struct BandRow {
196	        label: String,
197	        first: String,
198	        last: String,
199	        count: String,
200	        mean: String,
201	        adj_mean: String,
202	    }
203	
204	    let mut rows: Vec<BandRow> = Vec::new();
205	    for i in 0..n_bands {
206	        if band_count[i] == 0 {
207	            continue;
208	        }
209	        let mean_val = band_sum[i] as f64 / band_count[i] as f64;
210	        let adj_mean = (mean_val - adj).max(0.0);
211	        rows.push(BandRow {
212	            label: format!("{}-{}", boundary_names[i], boundary_names[i + 1]),
213	            first: fmt_commas(band_first[i]),
214	            last: fmt_commas(band_last[i]),
215	            count: fmt_commas(band_count[i]),
216	            mean: fmt_commas_f64(mean_val, 0),
217	            adj_mean: fmt_commas_f64(adj_mean, 0),
218	        });
219	    }
220	
221	    // Column widths from rendered strings.
222	    let label_w = rows.iter().map(|r| r.label.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
223	    let first_w = rows.iter().map(|r| r.first.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
224	    let last_w = rows.iter().map(|r| r.last.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
225	    let count_w = rows.iter().map(|r| r.count.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
226	    let mean_w = rows.iter().map(|r| r.mean.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
227	    let adj_w = rows.iter().map(|r| r.adj_mean.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
228	
229	    const INDENT: &str = "  ";
230	    const GAP: &str = "    ";
231	
232	    // Header row.
233	    let first_col = INDENT.len() + label_w + 1 + first_w;
234	    let last_gap = " ns".len() + GAP.len() + last_w;
235	    let count_gap = " ns".len() + GAP.len() + count_w;
236	    let mean_gap = GAP.len() + mean_w;
237	    let adj_gap = " ns".len() + GAP.len() + adj_w;
238	    println!(
239	        "{:>first_col$}{:>last_gap$}{:>count_gap$}{:>mean_gap$}{:>adj_gap$}",
240	        "first", "last", "count", "mean", "adjusted",
241	    );
242	
243	    for r in &rows {
244	        println!(
245	            "{INDENT}{:<label_w$} {:>first_w$} ns{GAP}{:>last_w$} ns{GAP}{:>count_w$}{GAP}{:>mean_w$} ns{GAP}{:>adj_w$} ns",
246	            r.label, r.first, r.last, r.count, r.mean, r.adj_mean,
247	        );
248	    }
249	
250	    // Whole-histogram summary. Aligned to mean/adjusted columns.
251	    let hist_mean = hist.mean();
252	    let hist_adj = (hist_mean - adj).max(0.0);
253	    let skip = first_w + " ns".len() + GAP.len() + last_w + " ns".len() + GAP.len() + count_w;
254	    println!(
255	        "{INDENT}{:<label_w$} {:>skip$}{GAP}{:>mean_w$} ns{GAP}{:>adj_w$} ns",
256	        "mean",
257	        "",
258	        fmt_commas_f64(hist_mean, 0),
259	        fmt_commas_f64(hist_adj, 0),
260	    );
261	    println!(
262	        "{INDENT}{:<label_w$} {:>skip$}{GAP}{:>mean_w$} ns",
263	        "stdev",
264	        "",
265	        fmt_commas_f64(hist.stdev(), 0),
266	    );
267	    println!();
268	}
269	
```

```json
  "new_string": "pub fn run_adaptive<B: Bench>(bench: &mut B, cfg: &RunCfg) -> (Histogram<u64>, u64, u64, f64) {\n    for _ in 0..WARMUP {\n        black_box(bench.step());\n    }\n\n    let step_cost_ns = estimate_step_cost(bench);\n    let framing_ns = cfg.overhead.framing_per_sample_ns.max(1.0);\n    let inner = cfg\n        .inner_override\n        .unwrap_or_else(|| pick_inner(step_cost_ns, framing_ns));\n\n    match cfg.outer_override {\n        Some(outer) => {\n            let (hist, duration_s) = run_counted(bench, outer, inner);\n            (hist, outer, inner, duration_s)\n        }\n        None => {\n            let (hist, duration_s) = run_timed(bench, cfg.target_seconds, inner);\n            let outer = hist.len();\n            (hist, outer, inner, duration_s)\n        }\n    }\n}"
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/iiac-perf/src/harness.rs",
  "old_string": "fn run_bench<B: Bench>(bench: &mut B, outer: u64, inner: u64) -> (Histogram<u64>, f64) {\n    // 1 ns to 60 s, 3 sig figs\n    let mut hist = Histogram::<u64>::new_with_bounds(1, 60_000_000_000, 3).unwrap();\n\n    let run_start = minstant::Instant::now();\n    for _ in 0..outer {\n        let start = minstant::Instant::now();\n        for _ in 0..inner {\n            black_box(bench.step());\n        }\n        let elapsed_ns = start.elapsed().as_nanos() as u64;\n        hist.record(round_elapsed(elapsed_ns, inner)).unwrap();\n    }\n    let duration_s = run_start.elapsed().as_nanos() as f64 / 1e9;\n\n    (hist, duration_s)\n}",
  "new_string": "fn run_counted<B: Bench>(bench: &mut B, outer: u64, inner: u64) -> (Histogram<u64>, f64) {\n    let mut hist = new_hist();\n    let run_start = minstant::Instant::now();\n    for _ in 0..outer {\n        record_sample(bench, inner, &mut hist);\n    }\n    let duration_s = run_start.elapsed().as_nanos() as f64 / 1e9;\n    (hist, duration_s)\n}\n\nfn run_timed<B: Bench>(bench: &mut B, target_seconds: f64, inner: u64) -> (Histogram<u64>, f64) {\n    let mut hist = new_hist();\n    let target_ns = (target_seconds * 1e9) as u128;\n    let run_start = minstant::Instant::now();\n    loop {\n        record_sample(bench, inner, &mut hist);\n        if run_start.elapsed().as_nanos() >= target_ns {\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    let duration_s = run_start.elapsed().as_nanos() as f64 / 1e9;\n    (hist, duration_s)\n}\n\nfn new_hist() -> Histogram<u64> {\n    // 1 ns to 60 s, 3 sig figs\n    Histogram::<u64>::new_with_bounds(1, 60_000_000_000, 3).unwrap()\n}\n\nfn record_sample<B: Bench>(bench: &mut B, inner: u64, hist: &mut Histogram<u64>) {\n    let start = minstant::Instant::now();\n    for _ in 0..inner {\n        black_box(bench.step());\n    }\n    let elapsed_ns = start.elapsed().as_nanos() as u64;\n    hist.record(round_elapsed(elapsed_ns, inner)).unwrap();\n}"
}
```

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01BKaBvQJNbkoCMtJmuB3xeJ
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/iiac-perf/notes/chores-01.md",
  "old_string": "Single-step bump 0.3.6 → 0.3.7.",
  "new_string": "Single-step bump 0.3.6 → 0.3.7.\n\n## Time-based outer loop + range column (0.4.0)\n\nTwo changes bundled into 0.4.0 since both affect measurement output.\n\n### Time-based outer loop\n\nThe outer loop now runs until wall-clock time exceeds the target\nduration (default 5s) instead of pre-computing an iteration count.\nThis is more honest — the old approach estimated step cost upfront\nand computed `outer = target_ns / step_cost`, but step cost varies\nduring a run (thermal throttling, scheduler noise, cache effects),\nso actual duration could drift from the target.\n\nThe time-based loop (`run_timed`) checks `elapsed >= target_ns`\nafter each sample. The actual outer count comes from `hist.len()`\nafter the loop — accurate by definition.\n\n`-o/--outer` still forces count-based mode (`run_counted`) for\nusers who want exact sample counts.\n\nRemoved `pick_outer`, `MIN_OUTER`, `MAX_OUTER` — no longer needed\nsince the default path is time-governed, not count-governed.\n\n### Range column\n\nAdded a `range` column to the histogram display, computed as\n`last - first + 1` per band. Shows band spread at a glance —\ntight range = clustered, wide range = high variance within that\npercentile slice. Positioned between `last` and `count`.\n\nThe `+1` is intentional for integer ns: `first=100, last=100` →\n`range=1` (one distinct value, not zero spread).\n\nSummary rows (mean, stdev) leave the range column blank.\n\nSingle-step bump 0.3.7 → 0.4.0."
}
```

All clean. And yes — I missed your "btw" message, sorry about that! What was it?

Here's the 0.4.0 summary:

- **Time-based outer loop**: default path now uses `run_timed` (loop until wall-clock exceeds target) instead of pre-computing iteration count. `-o` forces count-based `run_counted`. Removed `pick_outer`/`MIN_OUTER`/`MAX_OUTER`.
- **Range column**: `last - first + 1` per band, between `last` and `count`. Shows band spread at a glance.
- Notes and README updated.

Ready for your review.


```
153	
154	pub fn print_histogram(
155	    name: &str,
156	    outer: u64,
157	    inner: u64,
158	    duration_s: f64,
159	    hist: &Histogram<u64>,
160	    overhead: &Overhead,
161	) {
162	    // Header line: bench name + logfmt-style metadata. `adj` is the
163	    // apparatus overhead subtracted from each sample downstream.
164	    let adj = overhead.per_call_ns(inner);
165	    let total = outer * inner;
166	    println!(
167	        "{name} [duration={:.1}s outer={} inner={} calls={} adj/call={}ns]:",
168	        duration_s,
169	        fmt_commas(outer),
170	        inner,
171	        fmt_commas(total),
172	        fmt_commas_f64(adj, 2),
173	    );
174	
175	    // Band boundaries defined by percentiles. Each consecutive pair
176	    // forms one band; we iterate the histogram to compute per-band
177	    // stats (first, last, count, mean).
178	    let boundary_pcts: &[f64] = &[
179	        0.0, 0.01, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.40, 0.50, 0.60, 0.70, 0.80, 0.90, 0.99, 1.0,
180	    ];
181	    let boundary_names: &[&str] = &[
182	        "min", "p1", "p10", "p20", "p30", "p40", "p50", "p60", "p70", "p80", "p90", "p99", "max",
183	    ];
184	
185	    let n_bands = boundary_pcts.len() - 1;
186	    let sample_count = hist.len();
187	
188	    // Accumulate per-band stats by walking recorded histogram buckets.
189	    // Each bucket is assigned to the band containing its midpoint rank.
190	    let mut band_first = vec![u64::MAX; n_bands];
191	    let mut band_last = vec![0u64; n_bands];
192	    let mut band_count = vec![0u64; n_bands];
193	    let mut band_sum = vec![0u128; n_bands];
194	
195	    let mut cumulative = 0u64;
196	    for iv in hist.iter_recorded() {
197	        let value = iv.value_iterated_to();
198	        let count = iv.count_at_value();
199	        let mid_rank = (cumulative as f64 + count as f64 / 2.0) / sample_count as f64;
200	        let idx = boundary_pcts[1..]
201	            .iter()
202	            .position(|&b| mid_rank < b)
203	            .unwrap_or(n_bands - 1);
204	        band_first[idx] = band_first[idx].min(value);
205	        band_last[idx] = band_last[idx].max(value);
206	        band_count[idx] += count;
207	        band_sum[idx] += value as u128 * count as u128;
208	        cumulative += count;
209	    }
210	
211	    // Build rendered rows: (label, first, last, range, count, mean, adj_mean).
212	    struct BandRow {
213	        label: String,
214	        first: String,
215	        last: String,
216	        range: String,
217	        count: String,
218	        mean: String,
219	        adj_mean: String,
220	    }
221	
222	    let mut rows: Vec<BandRow> = Vec::new();
223	    for i in 0..n_bands {
224	        if band_count[i] == 0 {
225	            continue;
226	        }
227	        let mean_val = band_sum[i] as f64 / band_count[i] as f64;
228	        let adj_mean = (mean_val - adj).max(0.0);
229	        rows.push(BandRow {
230	            label: format!("{}-{}", boundary_names[i], boundary_names[i + 1]),
231	            first: fmt_commas(band_first[i]),
232	            last: fmt_commas(band_last[i]),
233	            range: fmt_commas(band_last[i] - band_first[i] + 1),
234	            count: fmt_commas(band_count[i]),
235	            mean: fmt_commas_f64(mean_val, 0),
236	            adj_mean: fmt_commas_f64(adj_mean, 0),
237	        });
238	    }
239	
240	    // Column widths from rendered strings.
241	    let label_w = rows.iter().map(|r| r.label.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
242	    let first_w = rows.iter().map(|r| r.first.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
243	    let last_w = rows.iter().map(|r| r.last.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
244	    let range_w = rows.iter().map(|r| r.range.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
245	    let count_w = rows.iter().map(|r| r.count.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
246	    let mean_w = rows.iter().map(|r| r.mean.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
247	    let adj_w = rows.iter().map(|r| r.adj_mean.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
248	
249	    const INDENT: &str = "  ";
250	    const GAP: &str = "    ";
251	
252	    // Header row.
253	    let first_col = INDENT.len() + label_w + 1 + first_w;
254	    let last_gap = " ns".len() + GAP.len() + last_w;
255	    let range_gap = " ns".len() + GAP.len() + range_w;
256	    let count_gap = " ns".len() + GAP.len() + count_w;
257	    let mean_gap = GAP.len() + mean_w;
258	    let adj_gap = " ns".len() + GAP.len() + adj_w;
259	    println!(
260	        "{:>first_col$}{:>last_gap$}{:>range_gap$}{:>count_gap$}{:>mean_gap$}{:>adj_gap$}",
261	        "first", "last", "range", "count", "mean", "adjusted",
262	    );
263	
264	    for r in &rows {
265	        println!(
266	            "{INDENT}{:<label_w$} {:>first_w$} ns{GAP}{:>last_w$} ns{GAP}{:>range_w$} ns{GAP}{:>count_w$}{GAP}{:>mean_w$} ns{GAP}{:>adj_w$} ns",
267	            r.label, r.first, r.last, r.range, r.count, r.mean, r.adj_mean,
268	        );
269	    }
270	
271	    // Whole-histogram summary. Aligned to mean/adjusted columns.
272	    let hist_mean = hist.mean();
273	    let hist_adj = (hist_mean - adj).max(0.0);
274	    let skip = first_w
275	        + " ns".len()
276	        + GAP.len()
277	        + last_w
278	        + " ns".len()
279	        + GAP.len()
280	        + range_w
281	        + " ns".len()
282	        + GAP.len()
283	        + count_w;
284	    println!(
285	        "{INDENT}{:<label_w$} {:>skip$}{GAP}{:>mean_w$} ns{GAP}{:>adj_w$} ns",
286	        "mean",
287	        "",
288	        fmt_commas_f64(hist_mean, 0),
289	        fmt_commas_f64(hist_adj, 0),
290	    );
291	    println!(
292	        "{INDENT}{:<label_w$} {:>skip$}{GAP}{:>mean_w$} ns",
293	        "stdev",
294	        "",
295	        fmt_commas_f64(hist.stdev(), 0),
296	    );
297	    println!();
298	}
299	
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/iiac-perf/notes/chores-01.md",
  "old_string": "## Time-based outer loop + range column (0.4.0)\n\nTwo changes bundled into 0.4.0 since both affect measurement output.\n\n### Time-based outer loop\n\nThe outer loop now runs until wall-clock time exceeds the target\nduration (default 5s) instead of pre-computing an iteration count.\nThis is more honest — the old approach estimated step cost upfront\nand computed `outer = target_ns / step_cost`, but step cost varies\nduring a run (thermal throttling, scheduler noise, cache effects),\nso actual duration could drift from the target.\n\nThe time-based loop (`run_timed`) checks `elapsed >= target_ns`\nafter each sample. The actual outer count comes from `hist.len()`\nafter the loop — accurate by definition.\n\n`-o/--outer` still forces count-based mode (`run_counted`) for\nusers who want exact sample counts.\n\nRemoved `pick_outer`, `MIN_OUTER`, `MAX_OUTER` — no longer needed\nsince the default path is time-governed, not count-governed.\n\n### Range column\n\nAdded a `range` column to the histogram display, computed as\n`last - first + 1` per band. Shows band spread at a glance —\ntight range = clustered, wide range = high variance within that\npercentile slice. Positioned between `last` and `count`.\n\nThe `+1` is intentional for integer ns: `first=100, last=100` →\n`range=1` (one distinct value, not zero spread).\n\nSummary rows (mean, stdev) leave the range column blank.\n\nSingle-step bump 0.3.7 → 0.4.0.",
  "new_string": "## Time-based outer loop (0.4.0)\n\nThe outer loop now runs until wall-clock time exceeds the target\nduration (default 5s) instead of pre-computing an iteration count.\nThis is more honest — the old approach estimated step cost upfront\nand computed `outer = target_ns / step_cost`, but step cost varies\nduring a run (thermal throttling, scheduler noise, cache effects),\nso actual duration could drift from the target.\n\n### Change\n\n- New `run_timed` loop checks `elapsed >= target_ns` after each\n  sample. Actual outer count comes from `hist.len()` — accurate\n  by definition.\n- `run_counted` preserves the old count-based loop for `-o/--outer`.\n- Shared `record_sample` + `new_hist` helpers factored out.\n- Removed `pick_outer`, `MIN_OUTER`, `MAX_OUTER` — no longer needed.\n- README updated to describe time-based default.\n\nSingle-step bump 0.3.7 → 0.4.0."
}
```

```
148	        None => (body, ""),
149	    };
150	    let int_num: u64 = int_part.parse().unwrap_or(0);
151	    format!("{sign}{}{frac_part}", fmt_commas(int_num))
152	}
153	
154	pub fn print_histogram(
155	    name: &str,
156	    outer: u64,
157	    inner: u64,
158	    duration_s: f64,
159	    hist: &Histogram<u64>,
160	    overhead: &Overhead,
161	) {
162	    // Header line: bench name + logfmt-style metadata. `adj` is the
163	    // apparatus overhead subtracted from each sample downstream.
164	    let adj = overhead.per_call_ns(inner);
165	    let total = outer * inner;
166	    println!(
167	        "{name} [duration={:.1}s outer={} inner={} calls={} adj/call={}ns]:",
168	        duration_s,
169	        fmt_commas(outer),
170	        inner,
171	        fmt_commas(total),
172	        fmt_commas_f64(adj, 2),
173	    );
174	
175	    // Band boundaries defined by percentiles. Each consecutive pair
176	    // forms one band; we iterate the histogram to compute per-band
177	    // stats (first, last, count, mean).
178	    let boundary_pcts: &[f64] = &[
179	        0.0, 0.01, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.40, 0.50, 0.60, 0.70, 0.80, 0.90, 0.99, 1.0,
180	    ];
181	    let boundary_names: &[&str] = &[
182	        "min", "p1", "p10", "p20", "p30", "p40", "p50", "p60", "p70", "p80", "p90", "p99", "max",
183	    ];
184	
185	    let n_bands = boundary_pcts.len() - 1;
186	    let sample_count = hist.len();
187	
188	    // Accumulate per-band stats by walking recorded histogram buckets.
189	    // Each bucket is assigned to the band containing its midpoint rank.
190	    let mut band_first = vec![u64::MAX; n_bands];
191	    let mut band_last = vec![0u64; n_bands];
192	    let mut band_count = vec![0u64; n_bands];
193	    let mut band_sum = vec![0u128; n_bands];
194	
195	    let mut cumulative = 0u64;
196	    for iv in hist.iter_recorded() {
197	        let value = iv.value_iterated_to();
198	        let count = iv.count_at_value();
199	        let mid_rank = (cumulative as f64 + count as f64 / 2.0) / sample_count as f64;
200	        let idx = boundary_pcts[1..]
201	            .iter()
202	            .position(|&b| mid_rank < b)
203	            .unwrap_or(n_bands - 1);
204	        band_first[idx] = band_first[idx].min(value);
205	        band_last[idx] = band_last[idx].max(value);
206	        band_count[idx] += count;
207	        band_sum[idx] += value as u128 * count as u128;
208	        cumulative += count;
209	    }
210	
211	    // Build rendered rows: (label, first, last, count, mean, adj_mean).
212	    struct BandRow {
213	        label: String,
214	        first: String,
215	        last: String,
216	        count: String,
217	        mean: String,
218	        adj_mean: String,
219	    }
220	
221	    let mut rows: Vec<BandRow> = Vec::new();
222	    for i in 0..n_bands {
223	        if band_count[i] == 0 {
224	            continue;
225	        }
226	        let mean_val = band_sum[i] as f64 / band_count[i] as f64;
227	        let adj_mean = (mean_val - adj).max(0.0);
228	        rows.push(BandRow {
229	            label: format!("{}-{}", boundary_names[i], boundary_names[i + 1]),
230	            first: fmt_commas(band_first[i]),
231	            last: fmt_commas(band_last[i]),
232	            count: fmt_commas(band_count[i]),
233	            mean: fmt_commas_f64(mean_val, 0),
234	            adj_mean: fmt_commas_f64(adj_mean, 0),
235	        });
236	    }
237	
238	    // Column widths from rendered strings.
239	    let label_w = rows.iter().map(|r| r.label.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
240	    let first_w = rows.iter().map(|r| r.first.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
241	    let last_w = rows.iter().map(|r| r.last.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
242	    let count_w = rows.iter().map(|r| r.count.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
243	    let mean_w = rows.iter().map(|r| r.mean.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
244	    let adj_w = rows.iter().map(|r| r.adj_mean.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
245	
246	    const INDENT: &str = "  ";
247	    const GAP: &str = "    ";
248	
249	    // Header row.
250	    let first_col = INDENT.len() + label_w + 1 + first_w;
251	    let last_gap = " ns".len() + GAP.len() + last_w;
252	    let count_gap = " ns".len() + GAP.len() + count_w;
253	    let mean_gap = GAP.len() + mean_w;
254	    let adj_gap = " ns".len() + GAP.len() + adj_w;
255	    println!(
256	        "{:>first_col$}{:>last_gap$}{:>count_gap$}{:>mean_gap$}{:>adj_gap$}",
257	        "first", "last", "count", "mean", "adjusted",
258	    );
259	
260	    for r in &rows {
261	        println!(
262	            "{INDENT}{:<label_w$} {:>first_w$} ns{GAP}{:>last_w$} ns{GAP}{:>count_w$}{GAP}{:>mean_w$} ns{GAP}{:>adj_w$} ns",
263	            r.label, r.first, r.last, r.count, r.mean, r.adj_mean,
264	        );
265	    }
266	
267	    // Whole-histogram summary. Aligned to mean/adjusted columns.
268	    let hist_mean = hist.mean();
269	    let hist_adj = (hist_mean - adj).max(0.0);
270	    let skip = first_w + " ns".len() + GAP.len() + last_w + " ns".len() + GAP.len() + count_w;
271	    println!(
272	        "{INDENT}{:<label_w$} {:>skip$}{GAP}{:>mean_w$} ns{GAP}{:>adj_w$} ns",
273	        "mean",
274	        "",
275	        fmt_commas_f64(hist_mean, 0),
276	        fmt_commas_f64(hist_adj, 0),
277	    );
278	    println!(
279	        "{INDENT}{:<label_w$} {:>skip$}{GAP}{:>mean_w$} ns",
280	        "stdev",
281	        "",
282	        fmt_commas_f64(hist.stdev(), 0),
283	    );
284	    println!();
285	}
286	
```

Positioned between `last` and `count`.\n\nThe `+1` is intentional for integer ns: `first=100, last=100` →\n`range=1` (one distinct value, not zero spread).\n\nSummary rows (mean, stdev) leave the range column blank.\n\nSingle-step bump 0.4.0 → 0.5.0."

Two separate points:

**README** — will update now.

**mean/stdev without p99-max** — I'd push back a bit. The p99-max band is arguably the most interesting one — it shows worst-case tail behavior (23,920 ns range, 47 ns mean vs 25 ns overall). Removing it would hide the outliers that pinning and scheduling cause. If the concern is that the tail skews the mean/stdev, an alternative would be a second pair of trimmed mean/stdev rows (e.g. "mean p1-p99" / "stdev p1-p99") that exclude both tails. But that's a separate feature — I'd leave 0.5.0 as-is and consider trimmed stats as a future todo if you want it.

76	
77	Each bench prints a per-percentile histogram in nanoseconds with
78	both the raw measurement and an adjusted column (apparatus overhead
79	subtracted). Apparatus = `framing_per_sample / INNER + loop_per_iter`,
80	calibrated once at startup via a two-point fit on an empty bench.
81	
82	Examples:
83	
84	```
85	iiac-perf                        # list available benches
86	iiac-perf all                    # every bench, default ~5s each
87	iiac-perf min-now -d 30          # one bench, 30s budget
88	iiac-perf all -D 30              # ~30s total split equally
89	iiac-perf mpsc-2t -i 1           # explicit single-call latency
90	iiac-perf mpsc-2t -i 100         # back-to-back rate
91	iiac-perf mpsc-2t --pin 0,1      # pinned, different physical cores
92	iiac-perf mpsc-2t --pin 0,12     # pinned, SMT siblings (contention)
93	```
94	
95	## Testing
96	
97	```
98	cargo test                                    # normal run
99	cargo test -- --nocapture                     # show eprintln diagnostics
100	taskset -c 0 cargo test -- --nocapture        # restrict to 1 CPU
101	```
102	
103	The `pin_current_can_switch_cores` test verifies that CPU pinning
104	works after a prior pin (the bug fixed in 0.3.6). It uses
105	`sched_getaffinity` to detect available CPUs, so under `taskset -c 0`

Aligned to mean/adjusted columns.\n    let hist_mean = hist.mean();\n    let hist_adj = (hist_mean - adj).max(0.0);\n    let skip = first_w\n        + \" ns\".len()\n        + GAP.len()\n        + last_w\n        + \" ns\".len()\n        + GAP.len()\n        + range_w\n        + \" ns\".len()\n        + GAP.len()\n        + count_w;\n    println!(\n        \"{INDENT}{:<label_w$} {:>skip$}{GAP}{:>mean_w$} ns{GAP}{:>adj_w$} ns\",\n        \"mean\",\n        \"\",\n        fmt_commas_f64(hist_mean, 0),\n        fmt_commas_f64(hist_adj, 0),\n    );\n    println!(\n        \"{INDENT}{:<label_w$} {:>skip$}{GAP}{:>mean_w$} ns\",\n        \"stdev\",\n        \"\",\n        fmt_commas_f64(hist.stdev(), 0),\n    );\n    println!();",
```json
  "new_string": "    // Whole-histogram summary. Aligned to mean/adjusted columns.\n    let hist_mean = hist.mean();\n    let hist_adj = (hist_mean - adj).max(0.0);\n    let skip = first_w\n        + \" ns\".len()\n        + GAP.len()\n        + last_w\n        + \" ns\".len()\n        + GAP.len()\n        + range_w\n        + \" ns\".len()\n        + GAP.len()\n        + count_w;\n    println!(\n        \"{INDENT}{:<label_w$} {:>skip$}{GAP}{:>mean_w$} ns{GAP}{:>adj_w$} ns\",\n        \"mean\",\n        \"\",\n        fmt_commas_f64(hist_mean, 0),\n        fmt_commas_f64(hist_adj, 0),\n    );\n    println!(\n        \"{INDENT}{:<label_w$} {:>skip$}{GAP}{:>mean_w$} ns\",\n        \"stdev\",\n        \"\",\n        fmt_commas_f64(hist.stdev(), 0),\n    );\n\n    // Trimmed mean/stdev (p1–p99, excluding min-p1 and p99-max bands).\n    let trim_count: u64 = band_count[1..n_bands - 1].iter().sum();\n    if trim_count > 0 {\n        let trim_sum: u128 = band_sum[1..n_bands - 1].iter().sum();\n        let trim_mean = trim_sum as f64 / trim_count as f64;\n        let trim_adj = (trim_mean - adj).max(0.0);\n\n        // Variance from per-band stats: sum of (value - mean)^2 * count.\n        // We approximate using band means since we don't have individual values.\n        // For a more accurate calculation, walk the histogram buckets again.\n        let mut trim_var_sum = 0.0f64;\n        let mut trim_var_count = 0u64;\n        let mut cum = 0u64;\n        for iv in hist.iter_recorded() {\n            let value = iv.value_iterated_to();\n            let count = iv.count_at_value();\n            let mid_rank = (cum as f64 + count as f64 / 2.0) / sample_count as f64;\n            let idx = boundary_pcts[1..]\n                .iter()\n                .position(|&b| mid_rank < b)\n                .unwrap_or(n_bands - 1);\n            // Include only inner bands (1..n_bands-1)\n            if idx > 0 && idx < n_bands - 1 {\n                let diff = value as f64 - trim_mean;\n                trim_var_sum += diff * diff * count as f64;\n                trim_var_count += count;\n            }\n            cum += count;\n        }\n        let trim_stdev = if trim_var_count > 1 {\n            (trim_var_sum / trim_var_count as f64).sqrt()\n        } else {\n            0.0\n        };\n\n        println!(\n            \"{INDENT}{:<label_w$} {:>skip$}{GAP}{:>mean_w$} ns{GAP}{:>adj_w$} ns\",\n            \"mean p1-p99\",\n            \"\",\n            fmt_commas_f64(trim_mean, 0),\n            fmt_commas_f64(trim_adj, 0),\n        );\n        println!(\n            \"{INDENT}{:<label_w$} {:>skip$}{GAP}{:>mean_w$} ns\",\n            \"stdev p1-p99\",\n            \"\",\n            fmt_commas_f64(trim_stdev, 0),\n        );\n    }\n    println!();"
}
```

> AGENT

That tells a great story — full stdev is 20,013 ns (dominated by tail outliers), trimmed stdev is just 614 ns. The core p1-p99 behavior is very tight.

Ready for your review.

> DEVELOPER

I was thinking of leaving min-p1 as that is "real" and it dispears when using --pin

> AGENT

Good point — min-p1 is real fast-path behavior (hot round-trips before parking kicks in), not noise. It disappears with `--pin` because pinned threads don't get scheduler jitter in the same way. Only p99-max has the kernel preemption / futex spikes that inflate stats.

So the trimmed stats should exclude only p99-max (last band), keeping min-p1.

> TOOL

```json
  "old_string": "    // Trimmed mean/stdev (p1–p99, excluding min-p1 and p99-max bands).\n    let trim_count: u64 = band_count[1..n_bands - 1].iter().sum();\n    if trim_count > 0 {\n        let trim_sum: u128 = band_sum[1..n_bands - 1].iter().sum();\n        let trim_mean = trim_sum as f64 / trim_count as f64;\n        let trim_adj = (trim_mean - adj).max(0.0);\n\n        // Variance from per-band stats: sum of (value - mean)^2 * count.\n        // We approximate using band means since we don't have individual values.\n        // For a more accurate calculation, walk the histogram buckets again.\n        let mut trim_var_sum = 0.0f64;\n        let mut trim_var_count = 0u64;\n        let mut cum = 0u64;\n        for iv in hist.iter_recorded() {\n            let value = iv.value_iterated_to();\n            let count = iv.count_at_value();\n            let mid_rank = (cum as f64 + count as f64 / 2.0) / sample_count as f64;\n            let idx = boundary_pcts[1..]\n                .iter()\n                .position(|&b| mid_rank < b)\n                .unwrap_or(n_bands - 1);\n            // Include only inner bands (1..n_bands-1)\n            if idx > 0 && idx < n_bands - 1 {\n                let diff = value as f64 - trim_mean;\n                trim_var_sum += diff * diff * count as f64;\n                trim_var_count += count;\n            }\n            cum += count;\n        }\n        let trim_stdev = if trim_var_count > 1 {\n            (trim_var_sum / trim_var_count as f64).sqrt()\n        } else {\n            0.0\n        };\n\n        println!(\n            \"{INDENT}{:<label_w$} {:>skip$}{GAP}{:>mean_w$} ns{GAP}{:>adj_w$} ns\",\n            \"mean p1-p99\",\n            \"\",\n            fmt_commas_f64(trim_mean, 0),\n            fmt_commas_f64(trim_adj, 0),\n        );\n        println!(\n            \"{INDENT}{:<label_w$} {:>skip$}{GAP}{:>mean_w$} ns\",\n            \"stdev p1-p99\",\n            \"\",\n            fmt_commas_f64(trim_stdev, 0),\n        );\n    }",
  "new_string": "    // Trimmed mean/stdev (min–p99, excluding p99-max band).\n    let trim_count: u64 = band_count[..n_bands - 1].iter().sum();\n    if trim_count > 0 {\n        let trim_sum: u128 = band_sum[..n_bands - 1].iter().sum();\n        let trim_mean = trim_sum as f64 / trim_count as f64;\n        let trim_adj = (trim_mean - adj).max(0.0);\n\n        // Variance: walk histogram buckets, include only non-tail bands.\n        let mut trim_var_sum = 0.0f64;\n        let mut trim_var_count = 0u64;\n        let mut cum = 0u64;\n        for iv in hist.iter_recorded() {\n            let value = iv.value_iterated_to();\n            let count = iv.count_at_value();\n            let mid_rank = (cum as f64 + count as f64 / 2.0) / sample_count as f64;\n            let idx = boundary_pcts[1..]\n                .iter()\n                .position(|&b| mid_rank < b)\n                .unwrap_or(n_bands - 1);\n            if idx < n_bands - 1 {\n                let diff = value as f64 - trim_mean;\n                trim_var_sum += diff * diff * count as f64;\n                trim_var_count += count;\n            }\n            cum += count;\n        }\n        let trim_stdev = if trim_var_count > 1 {\n            (trim_var_sum / trim_var_count as f64).sqrt()\n        } else {\n            0.0\n        };\n\n        println!(\n            \"{INDENT}{:<label_w$} {:>skip$}{GAP}{:>mean_w$} ns{GAP}{:>adj_w$} ns\",\n            \"mean min-p99\",\n            \"\",\n            fmt_commas_f64(trim_mean, 0),\n            fmt_commas_f64(trim_adj, 0),\n        );\n        println!(\n            \"{INDENT}{:<label_w$} {:>skip$}{GAP}{:>mean_w$} ns\",\n            \"stdev min-p99\",\n            \"\",\n            fmt_commas_f64(trim_stdev, 0),\n        );\n    }"
}
```
